class=\"mw-redirect\">Municipalities</a></th><td class=\"infobox-data\">54</td></tr></tbody></table><span class=\"mw-empty-elt\" about=\"#mwt2\"><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#image_alt\"><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#blank1_info_sec1\"><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#blank_info_sec1\"><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#blank1_name_sec1\"><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#blank_name_sec1\"><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#timezone1\"><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Pages_using_infobox_settlement_with_deprecated_parameters#image_skyline\"></span>\n<p id=\"mwCg\"><b id=\"mwCw\">Tlemcen</b> (<a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language\" title=\"Arabic language\" about=\"#mwt13\" typeof=\"mw:Transclusion\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwDA\" data-mw=\"{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;langx&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Langx&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ar&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ولاية تلمسان&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}\">Arabic</a><span about=\"#mwt13\">: </span><span lang=\"ar\" dir=\"rtl\" about=\"#mwt13\">ولاية تلمسان</span><link rel=\"mw:PageProp/Category\" href=\"./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text\" about=\"#mwt13\" id=\"mwDQ\">, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Berber_languages\" title=\"Berber languages\" id=\"mwDg\">Tamazight</a>: ⵜⴰⵡⵉⵍⴰⵢⵜ ⵏ ⵜⵍⴻⵎⵙⵉⵏ) is a <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Provinces_of_Algeria\" title=\"Provinces of Algeria\" id=\"mwDw\">province</a> (<i id=\"mwEA\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wilaya\" title=\"Wilaya\" id=\"mwEQ\">wilaya</a></i>) in northwestern <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Algeria\" title=\"Algeria\" id=\"mwEg\">Algeria</a>. It is located southwest of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oran\" title=\"Oran\" id=\"mwEw\">Oran</a>. According to the 2008 census, the province has a population of 945,525.<sup about=\"#mwt16\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-2\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-2&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-2\" id=\"mwFA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwFQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwFg\">[</span>2<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwFw\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<meta property=\"mw:PageProp/toc\" id=\"mwGA\" data-mw=\"{&quot;autoGenerated&quot;:true}\"></section><section data-mw-section-id=\"1\" id=\"mwGQ\" aria-labelledby=\"Geography\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Geography\">Geography</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Tlemcen_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1\" title=\"Edit section: Geography\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwGg\">Tlemcen Province lies among the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mountains_of_Tlemcen\" title=\"Mountains of Tlemcen\" id=\"mwGw\">Tlemcen Mountains</a> and the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trara_Mountains\" title=\"Trara Mountains\" id=\"mwHA\">Trara Mountains</a>. The province is home to a set of agricultural plains located in its southern and western parts. The <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hautes_Plaines\" title=\"Hautes Plaines\" id=\"mwHQ\">Algerian Steppes</a> run through its south.<sup about=\"#mwt20\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:0_3-0\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:0&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-:0-3&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:0-3\" id=\"mwHg\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwHw\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwIA\">[</span>3<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwIQ\">]</span></span></a></sup> Located in northwestern Algeria, it has an area of 9,017.69 km<sup id=\"mwIg\">2</sup>.<sup about=\"#mwt21\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:0_3-1\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:0&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:0-3\" id=\"mwIw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwJA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwJQ\">[</span>3<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwJg\">]</span></span></a></sup> It is bordered by <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morocco\" title=\"Morocco\" id=\"mwJw\">Morocco</a> to the west, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naâma_Province\" title=\"Naâma Province\" id=\"mwKA\">Naama</a> to the south, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sidi_Bel_Abbès_Province\" title=\"Sidi Bel Abbès Province\" id=\"mwKQ\">Sidi-Bel-Abbes</a> to the east, and the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_Sea\" title=\"Mediterranean Sea\" id=\"mwKg\">Mediterranean Sea</a> to the north. It has a Mediterranean coastline of about 120 kilometers.</p>\n\n<section data-mw-section-id=\"2\" id=\"mwKw\" aria-labelledby=\"Forestry\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"Forestry\">Forestry</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Tlemcen_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2\" title=\"Edit section: Forestry\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwLA\">Tlemcen Province has a forest cover of 225,000 ha, consisting of forest, scrub, and brush.<sup about=\"#mwt22\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:0_3-2\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:0&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:0-3\" id=\"mwLQ\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwLg\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwLw\">[</span>3<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwMA\">]</span></span></a></sup> With an afforestation rate of 24%, the province is considered to have a forestry vocation. However, forest cover is unevenly distributed; more than 80% of silvicultural potential is concentrated in the Tlemcen Mountains.</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwMQ\">The main forests are dominated by <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aleppo_pine\" title=\"Aleppo pine\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwMg\">aleppo pines</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quercus_ilex\" title=\"Quercus ilex\" id=\"mwMw\">holm oaks</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cedar_tree\" title=\"Cedar tree\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwNA\">thujas</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juniper\" title=\"Juniper\" id=\"mwNQ\">junipers</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cork_oaks\" title=\"Cork oaks\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwNg\">cork oaks</a>, and various other tree species. The most forested municipalities, with forest cover ranging from a third to two thirds of their territory, are located in the western parts of Tlemcen Mountains. (<a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Béni_Boussaïd_District\" title=\"Béni Boussaïd District\" id=\"mwNw\">Beni Boussaid</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bani_Sanus_(Algeria)\" title=\"Bani Sanus (Algeria)\" id=\"mwOA\">Beni Snous</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beni_Bahdel\" title=\"Beni Bahdel\" id=\"mwOQ\">Beni Bahdel</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Azails\" title=\"Azails\" id=\"mwOg\">Azails</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bouhlou,_Algeria\" title=\"Bouhlou, Algeria\" id=\"mwOw\">Bouhlou</a>)<sup about=\"#mwt23\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:0_3-3\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:0&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:0-3\" id=\"mwPA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwPQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwPg\">[</span>3<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwPw\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"3\" id=\"mwQA\" aria-labelledby=\"Climate\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"Climate\">Climate</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Tlemcen_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3\" title=\"Edit section: Climate\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<p id=\"mwQQ\">The province is dominated by a <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_climate\" title=\"Mediterranean climate\" id=\"mwQg\">Mediteranean climate</a> throughout its land mass with hot summers and cool/dry winters.<sup about=\"#mwt26\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:1_4-0\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-:1-4&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:1-4\" id=\"mwQw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwRA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwRQ\">[</span>4<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwRg\">]</span></span></a></sup> However, Tlemcen Province also includes two small pockets of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Semi-arid_climate\" title=\"Semi-arid climate\" id=\"mwRw\">semi-arid climates</a>, one hot and the other cold. Rainfall is generally subject to inter-annual irregularity.<sup about=\"#mwt27\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-:1_4-1\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;:1&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-:1-4\" id=\"mwSA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwSQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwSg\">[</span>4<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwSw\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n</section></section><section data-mw-section-id=\"4\" id=\"mwTA\" aria-labelledby=\"History\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"History\">History</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Tlemcen_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4\" title=\"Edit section: History\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<figure class=\"mw-default-size\" typeof=\"mw:File/Thumb\" id=\"mwTQ\"><a href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Barbadjani_tlemcen_algeria.jpg\" class=\"mw-file-description\" id=\"mwTg\"><img resource=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Barbadjani_tlemcen_algeria.jpg\" src=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e3/Barbadjani_tlemcen_algeria.jpg/250px-Barbadjani_tlemcen_algeria.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail\" decoding=\"async\" srcset=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e3/Barbadjani_tlemcen_algeria.jpg/500px-Barbadjani_tlemcen_algeria.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x\" data-file-width=\"1600\" data-file-height=\"1200\" data-file-type=\"bitmap\" height=\"188\" width=\"250\" class=\"mw-file-element\" id=\"mwTw\"></a><figcaption id=\"mwUA\">The Tlemcen Mountains</figcaption></figure>\n<p id=\"mwUQ\">Near the end of the 2nd century, the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_people\" title=\"Roman people\" id=\"mwUg\">Romans</a> founded the ancient military outpost of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pomaria_(Roman_city)\" title=\"Pomaria (Roman city)\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwUw\">Pomaria</a> on the present-day location of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tlemcen\" title=\"Tlemcen\" id=\"mwVA\">Tlemcen</a>. Over time, the region became the center of a large <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christians\" title=\"Christians\" id=\"mwVQ\">Christian</a> population lasting up to the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab_invasions\" title=\"Arab invasions\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwVg\">Arab invasions</a>. By the 7th century, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islam\" title=\"Islam\" id=\"mwVw\">Islam</a> was introduced into the region via <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abu_al-Muhajir_Dinar\" title=\"Abu al-Muhajir Dinar\" id=\"mwWA\">Abu al-Muhajir Dinar</a>, and the city of Agadir took Pomaria's place.<sup about=\"#mwt28\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-5\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-5&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-5\" id=\"mwWQ\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwWg\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwWw\">[</span>5<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwXA\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<p id=\"mwXQ\">The region then experienced the reigns of the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Almoravid_dynasty\" title=\"Almoravid dynasty\" id=\"mwXg\">Almoravid</a> and <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Almohad_Caliphate\" title=\"Almohad Caliphate\" id=\"mwXw\">Almohad</a> dynasties respectively.<sup about=\"#mwt29\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-6\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-6&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-6\" id=\"mwYA\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwYQ\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwYg\">[</span>6<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwYw\">]</span></span></a></sup> After the collapse of the Almohad dynasty, the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zayyanid_dynasty\" title=\"Zayyanid dynasty\" id=\"mwZA\">Zayyanid dynasty</a>, who ruled the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kingdom_of_Tlemcen\" title=\"Kingdom of Tlemcen\" id=\"mwZQ\">Kingdom of Tlemcen</a>, began their reign over the region. Shortly after, they made Tlemcen their capital.<sup about=\"#mwt30\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-7\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-7&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-7\" id=\"mwZg\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwZw\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwaA\">[</span>7<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwaQ\">]</span></span></a></sup> When the Zayyanids collapsed, the region became attached to the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Regency_of_Algiers\" title=\"Regency of Algiers\" id=\"mwag\">Regency of Algiers</a>.</p>\n\n<p id=\"mwaw\">At the start of the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French_conquest_of_Algeria\" title=\"French conquest of Algeria\" id=\"mwbA\">French conquest of Algeria</a>, the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Treaty_of_Tafna\" title=\"Treaty of Tafna\" id=\"mwbQ\">Treaty of Tafna</a> in 1837 elected <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emir_Abdelkader\" title=\"Emir Abdelkader\" id=\"mwbg\">Emir Abdelkader</a> as the governor of Tlemcen and surrounding areas.<sup about=\"#mwt31\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-8\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-8&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-8\" id=\"mwbw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwcA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwcQ\">[</span>8<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwcg\">]</span></span></a></sup> After the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Algerian_War\" title=\"Algerian War\" id=\"mwcw\">Algerian War</a>, the province was created from the French <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oran_(department)\" title=\"Oran (department)\" id=\"mwdA\">Oran Department</a> in 1974.</p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"5\" id=\"mwdQ\" aria-labelledby=\"Economy\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Economy\">Economy</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Tlemcen_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5\" title=\"Edit section: Economy\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<figure class=\"mw-default-size\" typeof=\"mw:File/Thumb\" id=\"mwdg\"><a href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Vue_depuis_la_plage_d'Agla_1,_Wilaya_de_Tlemcen.jpg\" class=\"mw-file-description\" id=\"mwdw\"><img resource=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Vue_depuis_la_plage_d'Agla_1,_Wilaya_de_Tlemcen.jpg\" src=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/ad/Vue_depuis_la_plage_d%27Agla_1%2C_Wilaya_de_Tlemcen.jpg/250px-Vue_depuis_la_plage_d%27Agla_1%2C_Wilaya_de_Tlemcen.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail\" decoding=\"async\" srcset=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/ad/Vue_depuis_la_plage_d%27Agla_1%2C_Wilaya_de_Tlemcen.jpg/500px-Vue_depuis_la_plage_d%27Agla_1%2C_Wilaya_de_Tlemcen.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x\" data-file-width=\"5770\" data-file-height=\"3847\" data-file-type=\"bitmap\" height=\"167\" width=\"250\" class=\"mw-file-element\" id=\"mweA\"></a><figcaption id=\"mweQ\">Agla Beach, Honaïne, in Tlemcen Province</figcaption></figure>\n<p id=\"mweg\">Agriculture is an important economic sector in the province. The plains of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maghnia\" title=\"Maghnia\" id=\"mwew\">Maghnia</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Remchi\" title=\"Remchi\" id=\"mwfA\">Remchi</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hennaya\" title=\"Hennaya\" id=\"mwfQ\">Hennaya</a>, and the basins of <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beni_Ouarsous\" title=\"Beni Ouarsous\" id=\"mwfg\">Beni Ouarsous</a> are the main producers of agricultural products such as <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Potato\" title=\"Potato\" id=\"mwfw\">potatoes</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fruit\" title=\"Fruit\" id=\"mwgA\">fruits</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cereal\" title=\"Cereal\" id=\"mwgQ\">cereals</a>, <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vegetable\" title=\"Vegetable\" id=\"mwgg\">vegetables</a>, et ceteta. The province is known for the manufacturing of textiles, leather, carpet, cement, and fuel in major cities such as Tlemcen.<sup about=\"#mwt34\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-9\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-9&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-9\" id=\"mwgw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwhA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwhQ\">[</span>9<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwhg\">]</span></span></a></sup> The Souk Tleta Seawater Desalination Plant was inaugurated in April 2011 by the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Water_Resources_(Algeria)\" title=\"Ministry of Water Resources (Algeria)\" class=\"mw-redirect\" id=\"mwhw\">Ministry of Water Resources</a> for President <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abdelaziz_Bouteflika\" title=\"Abdelaziz Bouteflika\" id=\"mwiA\">Abdelaziz Boutefilka</a>'s visit to Tlemcen.<sup about=\"#mwt37\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-10\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-10&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-10\" id=\"mwiQ\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwig\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwiw\">[</span>10<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwjA\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n<p id=\"mwjQ\">With a coastline overlooking the Mediterranean, the province has experienced a resurgence of tourist activity since the <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2000s\" title=\"2000s\" id=\"mwjg\">2000s</a> due to the creation of tourist complexes on the province's beaches. In 2012, the province had fifty-eight hotels and 1,896 rooms within them combined.<sup about=\"#mwt40\" class=\"mw-ref reference\" id=\"cite_ref-11\" rel=\"dc:references\" typeof=\"mw:Extension/ref\" data-mw=\"{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-11&quot;}}\"><a href=\"#cite_note-11\" id=\"mwjw\"><span class=\"mw-reflink-text\" id=\"mwkA\"><span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwkQ\">[</span>11<span class=\"cite-bracket\" id=\"mwkg\">]</span></span></a></sup></p>\n\n</section><section data-mw-section-id=\"6\" id=\"mwkw\" aria-labelledby=\"Administrative_divisions\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading2\"><h2 id=\"Administrative_divisions\">Administrative divisions</h2><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">[</span><a href=\"/w/index.php?title=Tlemcen_Province&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6\" title=\"Edit section: Administrative divisions\"><span>edit</span></a><span class=\"mw-editsection-bracket\">]</span></span></div>\n<figure class=\"mw-default-size\" typeof=\"mw:File/Thumb\" id=\"mwlA\"><a href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:DZ-13-00_-_Wilaya_Tlemcen_-_bullet.svg\" class=\"mw-file-description\" id=\"mwlQ\"><img resource=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:DZ-13-00_-_Wilaya_Tlemcen_-_bullet.svg\" src=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/f7/DZ-13-00_-_Wilaya_Tlemcen_-_bullet.svg/250px-DZ-13-00_-_Wilaya_Tlemcen_-_bullet.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail\" decoding=\"async\" srcset=\"//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/f7/DZ-13-00_-_Wilaya_Tlemcen_-_bullet.svg/500px-DZ-13-00_-_Wilaya_Tlemcen_-_bullet.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x\" data-file-width=\"1199\" data-file-height=\"1077\" data-file-type=\"drawing\" height=\"225\" width=\"250\" class=\"mw-file-element\" id=\"mwlg\"></a><figcaption id=\"mwlw\">Organization of Tlemcen Province</figcaption></figure>\n<p id=\"mwmA\">The province is divided into 20 <a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Districts_of_Algeria\" title=\"Districts of Algeria\" id=\"mwmQ\">districts</a> (<i id=\"mwmg\"><a rel=\"mw:WikiLink\" href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daïra\" title=\"Daïra\" id=\"mwmw\">daïras</a></i>) which have existed since independence in 1962:</p>\n\n<section data-mw-section-id=\"7\" id=\"mwnA\" aria-labelledby=\"Districts\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"Districts\">Districts</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span data-mw-section-id=\"8\" id=\"mwnw\" aria-labelledby=\"Communes\"><div class=\"mw-heading mw-heading3\"><h3 id=\"Communes\">Communes</h3><span class=\"mw-editsection\"><span data-mw=\"{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;div
